# Plugins · A · part 6

1,261 entries, ai-model-research to aix. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ai-model-research**: Research AI models: discover, filter, compare and evaluate, across OpenRouter and the Hugging Face Hub. Built on small unofficial read-only CLIs: orexplorer for OpenRouter (capabilities, batch support, prices, providers, new and trending models, benchmarks) and hubexplorer for the Hugging Face Hub (task-first discovery filtered by format, language, local app, inference provider, model type, size and licence, with every sort order). Skills cover lookup, capability filtering, recommendation, comparison, deep evaluation, cost projection, alternatives, task-first HF model research, transcription… ([danielrosehill/AI-Model-Research-Plugin](https://github.com/danielrosehill/AI-Model-Research-Plugin) · 26 skills)
